  63 |             If Not Setup.IsUnset("LaunchArgumentIndie") Then
  64 |                 Log("[Start] 从老 PCL 迁移版本隔离")
  65 |                 Setup.Set("LaunchArgumentIndieV2", Setup.Get("LaunchArgumentIndie"))
  66 |             ElseIf Not Setup.IsUnset("WindowHeight") Then
  67 |                 Log("[Start] 从老 PCL 升级，但此前未调整版本隔离，使用老的版本隔离默认值")
  68 |                 Setup.Set("LaunchArgumentIndieV2", Setup.GetDefault("LaunchArgumentIndie"))
  69 |             Else
  70 |                 Log("[Start] 全新的 PCL，使用新的版本隔离默认值")
  71 |                 Setup.Set("LaunchArgumentIndieV2", Setup.GetDefault("LaunchArgumentIndieV2"))
  72 |             End If
  73 |         End If
  74 |         Setup.Load("UiLauncherTheme")
  75 |         '注册拖拽事件（不能直接加 Handles，否则没用；#6340）
  76 |         [AddHandler](DragDrop.DragEnterEvent, New DragEventHandler(AddressOf HandleDrag), handledEventsToo:=True)
  77 |         [AddHandler](DragDrop.DragOverEvent, New DragEventHandler(AddressOf HandleDrag), handledEventsToo:=True)
  78 |         '加载 UI
  79 |         InitializeComponent()
  80 |         Opacity = 0
  81 |         Try
  82 |             Height = Setup.Get("WindowHeight")
  83 |             Width = Setup.Get("WindowWidth")
  84 |         Catch ex As Exception '修复 #2019
  85 |             Log(ex, "读取窗口默认大小失败", LogLevel.Hint)
  86 |             Height = MinHeight + 100
  87 |             Width = MinWidth + 100
  88 |         End Try
  89 |         ''开启管理员权限下的文件拖拽，但下列代码也没用（#2531）
  90 |         'If IsAdmin() Then
  91 |         '    Log("[Start] PCL 正以管理员权限运行")
  92 |         '    ChangeWindowMessageFilter(&H233, 1)
  93 |         '    ChangeWindowMessageFilter(&H4A, 1)
  94 |         '    ChangeWindowMessageFilter(&H49, 1)
  95 |         'End If
  96 |         '切换到首页
  97 |         If Not IsNothing(FrmLaunchLeft.Parent) Then FrmLaunchLeft.SetValue(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, Nothing)
  98 |         If Not IsNothing(FrmLaunchRight.Parent) Then FrmLaunchRight.SetValue(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, Nothing)
  99 |         PanMainLeft.Child = FrmLaunchLeft
 100 |         PageLeft = FrmLaunchLeft
 101 |         PanMainRight.Child = FrmLaunchRight
 102 |         PageRight = FrmLaunchRight
 103 |         FrmLaunchRight.PageState = MyPageRight.PageStates.ContentStay
 104 |         '调试模式提醒
 105 |         If ModeDebug Then Hint("[调试模式] PCL 正以调试模式运行，这可能会导致性能下降，若无必要请不要开启！")
 106 |         '尽早执行的加载池
 107 |         McFolderListLoader.Start(0) '为了让下载已存在文件检测可以正常运行，必须跑一次；为了让启动按钮尽快可用，需要尽早执行；为了与 PageLaunchLeft 联动，需要为 0 而不是 GetUuid
 108 | 
 109 |         Log("[Start] 第二阶段加载用时：" & TimeUtils.GetTimeTick() - ApplicationStartTick & " ms")
 110 |         '注册生命周期状态事件
 111 |         Lifecycle.When(LifecycleState.WindowCreated, AddressOf FormMain_Loaded)
 112 |     End Sub

 166 |         AniStart({

 169 |             AaDouble(Sub(i) TransformPos.Y += i, -TransformPos.Y, 600, 100, New AniEaseOutBack(AniEasePower.Weak)),
 170 |             AaDouble(Sub(i) TransformRotate.Angle += i, -TransformRotate.Angle, 500, 100, New AniEaseOutBack(AniEasePower.Weak)),
 171 |             AaCode(

 173 |                 PanBack.RenderTransform = Nothing

 175 |                 Log($"[System] DPI：{DPI}，系统版本：{Environment.OSVersion.VersionString}，PCL 位置：{ExePathWithName}")
 176 |             End Sub, , True)

 181 |         '加载池
 182 |         RunInNewThread(

 187 | #If DEBUG Then

 200 |                          "特殊版本提示", "我清楚我在做什么", "打开最新版下载页并退出", IsWarn:=True,

 228 |                                      "你可以随时在启动器设置中调整这项设置。", "参与 PCL CE 软硬件调查", "同意", "拒绝")
 229 |                     Case 1

 250 |             End Try

 252 |             GetSystemInfo()

 257 |     '根据打开次数触发的事件
 258 |     Private Sub RunCountSub()
 259 |         Setup.Set("SystemCount", Setup.Get("SystemCount") + 1)
 260 |         If Setup.Get("SystemCount") >= 99 Then
 261 |             If ThemeUnlock(6, False) Then
 262 |                 MyMsgBox("你已经打开了 99 次 PCL 社区版啦，感谢你长期以来的支持！" & vbCrLf &
 263 |                          "隐藏主题 铁杆粉 未解锁！社区版不包含隐藏主题！", "提示")
 264 |             End If
 265 |         End If
 266 |     End Sub
 267 |     '升级与降级事件
 268 |     Private Sub UpgradeSub(LastVersionCode As Integer)
 269 |         Log("[Start] 版本号从 " & LastVersionCode & " 升高到 " & VersionCode)
 270 |         Setup.Set("SystemLastVersionReg", VersionCode)
 271 |         '检查有记录的最高版本号
 272 |         Dim LowerVersionCode As Integer
 273 | #If BETA Then
 274 |         LowerVersionCode = Setup.Get("SystemHighestBetaVersionReg")
 275 |         If LowerVersionCode < VersionCode Then
 276 |             Setup.Set("SystemHighestBetaVersionReg", VersionCode)
 277 |             Log("[Start] 最高版本号从 " & LowerVersionCode & " 升高到 " & VersionCode)
 278 |         End If
 279 | #Else
 280 |         LowerVersionCode = Setup.Get("SystemHighestAlphaVersionReg")
 281 |         If LowerVersionCode < VersionCode Then
 282 |             Setup.Set("SystemHighestAlphaVersionReg", VersionCode)
 283 |             Log("[Start] 最高版本号从 " & LowerVersionCode & " 升高到 " & VersionCode)
 284 |         End If
 285 | #End If
 286 |         '被移除的窗口设置选项
 287 |         If Setup.Get("LaunchArgumentWindowType") = 5 Then Setup.Set("LaunchArgumentWindowType", 1)
 288 |         '修改主题设置项名称
 289 |         If LowerVersionCode <= 207 Then
 290 |             Dim UnlockedTheme As New List(Of String) From {"2"}
 291 |             UnlockedTheme.AddRange(New List(Of String)(Setup.Get("UiLauncherThemeHide").ToString.Split("|")))
 292 |             UnlockedTheme.AddRange(New List(Of String)(Setup.Get("UiLauncherThemeHide2").ToString.Split("|")))
 293 |             Setup.Set("UiLauncherThemeHide2", Join(UnlockedTheme.Distinct.ToList, "|"))
 294 |         End If
 295 |         '重置欧皇彩
 296 |         If LastVersionCode <= 115 AndAlso Setup.Get("UiLauncherThemeHide2").ToString.Split("|").Contains("13") Then
 297 |             Dim UnlockedTheme As New List(Of String)(Setup.Get("UiLauncherThemeHide2").ToString.Split("|"))
 298 |             UnlockedTheme.Remove("13")
 299 |             Setup.Set("UiLauncherThemeHide2", Join(UnlockedTheme, "|"))
 300 |             MyMsgBox("由于新版 PCL 修改了欧皇彩的解锁方式，你需要重新解锁欧皇彩。" & vbCrLf &
 301 |                      "多谢各位的理解啦！", "重新解锁提醒")
 302 |         End If
 303 |         '重置滑稽彩
 304 |         If LastVersionCode <= 152 AndAlso Setup.Get("UiLauncherThemeHide2").ToString.Split("|").Contains("12") Then
 305 |             Dim UnlockedTheme As New List(Of String)(Setup.Get("UiLauncherThemeHide2").ToString.Split("|"))
 306 |             UnlockedTheme.Remove("12")
 307 |             Setup.Set("UiLauncherThemeHide2", Join(UnlockedTheme, "|"))
 308 |             MyMsgBox("由于新版 PCL 修改了滑稽彩的解锁方式，你需要重新解锁滑稽彩。" & vbCrLf &
 309 |                      "多谢各位的理解啦！", "重新解锁提醒")
 310 |         End If
 311 |         '移动自定义皮肤
 312 |         If LastVersionCode <= 161 AndAlso File.Exists(ExePath & "PCL\CustomSkin.png") AndAlso Not File.Exists(PathAppdata & "CustomSkin.png") Then
 313 |             CopyFile(ExePath & "PCL\CustomSkin.png", PathAppdata & "CustomSkin.png")
 314 |             Log("[Start] 已移动离线自定义皮肤 (162)")
 315 |         End If
 316 |         If LastVersionCode <= 263 AndAlso File.Exists(PathTemp & "CustomSkin.png") AndAlso Not File.Exists(PathAppdata & "CustomSkin.png") Then
 317 |             CopyFile(PathTemp & "CustomSkin.png", PathAppdata & "CustomSkin.png")
 318 |             Log("[Start] 已移动离线自定义皮肤 (264)")
 319 |         End If
 320 |         '解除帮助页面的隐藏
 321 |         If LastVersionCode <= 205 Then
 322 |             Setup.Set("UiHiddenOtherHelp", False)
 323 |             Log("[Start] 已解除帮助页面的隐藏")
 324 |         End If
 325 |         '迁移旧版用户档案
 326 |         If LastVersionCode <= 368 Then
 327 |             RunInNewThread(Sub() MigrateOldProfile())
 328 |         End If
 329 |         'Mod 命名设置迁移
 330 |         If Not Setup.IsUnset("ToolDownloadTranslate") AndAlso Setup.IsUnset("ToolDownloadTranslateV2") Then
 331 |             Setup.Set("ToolDownloadTranslateV2", Setup.Get("ToolDownloadTranslate") + 1)
 332 |             Log("[Start] 已从老版本迁移 Mod 命名设置")
 333 |         End If
 334 |         '社区版提示
 335 |         If Not Setup.Get("UiLauncherCEHint") Then ShowCEAnnounce(True)
 336 |         '输出更新日志
 337 |         If LastVersionCode <= 0 Then Return
 338 |         If LowerVersionCode >= VersionCode Then Return
 339 |         ShowUpdateLog()
 340 |     End Sub
 341 |     Private Sub DowngradeSub(LastVersionCode As Integer)
 342 |         Log("[Start] 版本号从 " & LastVersionCode & " 降低到 " & VersionCode)
 343 |         Setup.Set("SystemLastVersionReg", VersionCode)
 344 |     End Sub

 361 |     '关闭
 362 |     Private Sub FormMain_Closing(sender As Object, e As CancelEventArgs) Handles Me.Closing
 363 |         EndProgram(True)
 364 |         e.Cancel = True
 365 |     End Sub
 366 |     ''' <summary>
 367 |     ''' 正常关闭程序。程序将在执行此方法后约 0.3s 退出。
 368 |     ''' </summary>
 369 |     ''' <param name="SendWarning">是否在还有下载任务未完成时发出警告。</param>
 370 |     Public Sub EndProgram(SendWarning As Boolean)
 371 |         '发出警告
 372 |         If SendWarning AndAlso HasDownloadingTask() Then
 373 |             If MyMsgBox("还有下载任务尚未完成，是否确定退出？", "提示", "确定", "取消") = 1 Then
 374 |                 '强行结束下载任务
 375 |                 RunInNewThread(
 376 |                 Sub()
 377 |                     Log("[System] 正在强行停止任务")
 378 |                     For Each Task As LoaderBase In LoaderTaskbar.ToList()
 379 |                         Task.Abort()
 380 |                     Next
 381 |                 End Sub, "强行停止下载任务")
 382 |             Else
 383 |                 Return
 384 |             End If
 385 |         End If
 386 |         '关闭联机大厅
 387 |         LobbyController.Close()
 388 |         '存储上次使用的档案编号
 389 |         SaveProfile()
 390 |         '关闭
 391 |         RunInUiWait(
 392 |         Sub()
 393 |             '清理视频背景
 394 |             VideoBack.Stop()
 395 |             VideoBack.Source = Nothing
 396 |             VideoBack.Close()
 397 |             IsHitTestVisible = False
 398 |             If PanBack.RenderTransform Is Nothing Then
 399 |                 Dim TransformPos As New TranslateTransform(0, 0)
 400 |                 Dim TransformRotate As New RotateTransform(0)
 401 |                 Dim TransformScale As New ScaleTransform(1, 1)
 402 |                 PanBack.RenderTransform = New TransformGroup() With {.Children = New TransformCollection({TransformRotate, TransformPos, TransformScale})}
 403 |                 AniStart({
 404 |                     AaOpacity(Me, -Opacity, 140, 40, New AniEaseOutFluent(AniEasePower.Weak)),
 405 |                     AaDouble(
 406 |                     Sub(i)
 407 |                         TransformScale.ScaleX += i
 408 |                         TransformScale.ScaleY += i
 409 |                     End Sub, 0.88 - TransformScale.ScaleX, 180),
 410 |                     AaDouble(Sub(i) TransformPos.Y += i, 20 - TransformPos.Y, 180, 0, New AniEaseOutFluent(AniEasePower.Weak)),
 411 |                     AaDouble(Sub(i) TransformRotate.Angle += i, 0.6 - TransformRotate.Angle, 180, 0, New AniEaseInoutFluent(AniEasePower.Weak)),
 412 |                     AaCode(
 413 |                     Sub()
 414 |                         IsHitTestVisible = False
 415 |                         Top = -10000
 416 |                         ShowInTaskbar = False
 417 |                     End Sub, 210),
 418 |                     AaCode(AddressOf EndProgramForce, 230)
 419 |                 }, "Form Close")
 420 |             Else
 421 |                 EndProgramForce()
 422 |             End If
 423 |             Log("[System] 收到关闭指令")
 424 |         End Sub)
 425 |     End Sub
 426 |     Private Shared IsLogShown As Boolean = False
 427 |     Public Shared Sub EndProgramForce(Optional ReturnCode As ProcessReturnValues = ProcessReturnValues.Success)
 428 |         On Error Resume Next
 429 |         '关闭联机大厅
 430 |         LobbyController.Close()
 431 |         IsProgramEnded = True
 432 |         AniControlEnabled += 1
 433 |         If IsUpdateWaitingRestart Then UpdateRestart(False)
 434 |         If ReturnCode = ProcessReturnValues.Exception Then
 435 |             If Not IsLogShown Then
 436 |                 FeedbackInfo()
 437 |                 Log("请在 https://github.com/PCL-Community/PCL2-CE/issues 提交错误报告，以便于社区解决此问题！（这也有可能是原版 PCL 的问题）")
 438 |                 IsLogShown = True
 439 |                 ShellOnly(LogWrapper.CurrentLogger.LogFiles.Last())
 440 |             End If
 441 |             Thread.Sleep(500) '防止 PCL 在记事本打开前就被掐掉
 442 |         End If
 443 |         Log("[System] 程序已退出，返回值：" & GetStringFromEnum(ReturnCode))
 444 |         'If ReturnCode <> ProcessReturnValues.Success Then Environment.Exit(ReturnCode)
 445 |         'Process.GetCurrentProcess.Kill()
 446 |         Lifecycle.ForceShutdown(ReturnCode)
 447 |     End Sub
 448 |     Private Sub BtnTitleClose_Click(sender As Object, e As RoutedEventArgs) Handles BtnTitleClose.Click
 449 |         EndProgram(True)
 450 |     End Sub

 458 |     '改变大小
 459 |     ''' <summary>
 460 |     ''' 是否可以向注册表储存尺寸改变信息。以此避免初始化时误储存。
 461 |     ''' </summary>
 462 |     Public IsSizeSaveable As Boolean = False
 463 |     Private Sub FormMain_SizeChanged() Handles Me.SizeChanged, Me.Loaded
 464 |         If IsSizeSaveable Then
 465 |             Setup.Set("WindowHeight", Height)
 466 |             Setup.Set("WindowWidth", Width)
 467 |         End If
 468 |         RectForm.Rect = New Rect(0, 0, BorderForm.ActualWidth, BorderForm.ActualHeight)
 469 |         PanForm.Width = BorderForm.ActualWidth + 0.001
 470 |         PanForm.Height = BorderForm.ActualHeight + 0.001
 471 |         PanMain.Width = PanForm.Width
 472 |         PanMain.Height = Math.Max(0, PanForm.Height - PanTitle.ActualHeight)
 473 |         VideoBack.Width = PanForm.Width
 474 |         VideoBack.Height = PanForm.Height
 475 |         If WindowState = WindowState.Maximized Then WindowState = WindowState.Normal '修复 #1938
 476 |     End Sub
 477 | 
 478 |     '标题栏改变大小
 479 |     Private Sub PanTitle_SizeChanged() Handles PanTitleLeft.SizeChanged
 480 |         If PanTitleMain.ColumnDefinitions(0).ActualWidth - 30 <= 0 Then
 481 |             PanTitleLeft.ColumnDefinitions(0).MaxWidth = 0
 482 |         Else
 483 |             PanTitleLeft.ColumnDefinitions(0).MaxWidth = PanTitleMain.ColumnDefinitions(0).ActualWidth - 30
 484 |         End If
 485 |     End Sub

 511 |     '按键事件
 512 |     Private Sub FormMain_KeyDown(sender As Object, e As KeyEventArgs) Handles Me.KeyDown
 513 |         If e.IsRepeat Then Return
 514 |         '调用弹窗：回车选择第一个，Esc 选择最后一个
 515 |         If PanMsg.Children.Count > 0 Then
 516 |             If e.Key = Key.Enter Then
 517 |                 CType(PanMsg.Children(0), Object).Btn1_Click()
 518 |                 Return
 519 |             ElseIf e.Key = Key.Escape Then
 520 |                 Dim Msg As Object = PanMsg.Children(0)
 521 |                 If TypeOf Msg IsNot MyMsgInput AndAlso TypeOf Msg IsNot MyMsgSelect AndAlso Msg.Btn3.Visibility = Visibility.Visible Then
 522 |                     Msg.Btn3_Click()
 523 |                 ElseIf Msg.Btn2.Visibility = Visibility.Visible Then
 524 |                     Msg.Btn2_Click()
 525 |                 Else
 526 |                     Msg.Btn1_Click()
 527 |                 End If
 528 |                 Return
 529 |             End If
 530 |         End If
 531 |         '按 ESC 返回上一级
 532 |         If e.Key = Key.Escape Then TriggerPageBack()
 533 |         '更改隐藏实例可见性
 534 |         If e.Key = Key.F11 AndAlso PageCurrent = FormMain.PageType.InstanceSelect Then
 535 |             FrmSelectRight.ShowHidden = Not FrmSelectRight.ShowHidden
 536 |             LoaderFolderRun(McInstanceListLoader, PathMcFolder, LoaderFolderRunType.ForceRun, MaxDepth:=1, ExtraPath:="versions\")
 537 |             Return
 538 |         End If
 539 |         '更改功能隐藏可见性
 540 |         If e.Key = Key.F12 Then
 541 |             PageSetupUI.HiddenForceShow = Not PageSetupUI.HiddenForceShow
 542 |             If PageSetupUI.HiddenForceShow Then
 543 |                 Hint("功能隐藏设置已暂时关闭！", HintType.Finish)
 544 |             Else
 545 |                 Hint("功能隐藏设置已重新开启！", HintType.Finish)
 546 |             End If
 547 |             PageSetupUI.HiddenRefresh()
 548 |             Return
 549 |         End If
 550 |         '按 F5 刷新页面
 551 |         If e.Key = Key.F5 Then
 552 |             If TypeOf PageLeft Is IRefreshable Then CType(PageLeft, IRefreshable).Refresh()
 553 |             If TypeOf PageRight Is IRefreshable Then CType(PageRight, IRefreshable).Refresh()
 554 |             Return
 555 |         End If
 556 |         '调用启动游戏
 557 |         If e.Key = Key.Enter AndAlso PageCurrent = FormMain.PageType.Launch Then
 558 |             If IsAprilEnabled AndAlso Not IsAprilGiveup Then
 559 |                 Hint("木大！")
 560 |             Else
 561 |                 FrmLaunchLeft.LaunchButtonClick()
 562 |             End If
 563 |         End If
 564 |         '修复按下 Alt 后误认为弹出系统菜单导致的冻结
 565 |         If e.SystemKey = Key.LeftAlt OrElse e.SystemKey = Key.RightAlt Then e.Handled = True
 566 |     End Sub
 567 |     Private Sub FormMain_MouseDown(sender As Object, e As MouseButtonEventArgs) Handles Me.MouseDown
 568 |         '鼠标侧键返回上一级
 569 |         If FrmMain.PanMsg.Children.Count > 0 OrElse WaitingMyMsgBox.Any Then Return '弹窗中（#5513）
 570 |         If e.ChangedButton = MouseButton.XButton1 OrElse e.ChangedButton = MouseButton.XButton2 Then TriggerPageBack()
 571 |     End Sub
 572 |     Private Sub TriggerPageBack()
 573 |         If PageCurrent = PageType.Download AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.DownloadInstall AndAlso FrmDownloadInstall.IsInSelectPage Then
 574 |             FrmDownloadInstall.ExitSelectPage()
 575 |         ElseIf PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionInstall AndAlso FrmInstanceInstall.IsInSelectPage Then
 576 |             FrmInstanceInstall.ExitSelectPage()
 577 |         Else
 578 |             PageBack()
 579 |         End If
 580 |     End Sub

 582 |     '切回窗口
 583 |     Private Sub FormMain_Activated() Handles Me.Activated
 584 |         Try
 585 |             If Setup.Get("ToolDownloadClipboard") Then CompClipboard.GetClipboardResource()
 586 |             If PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionMod Then
 587 |                 'Mod 管理自动刷新
 588 |                 FrmInstanceMod.ReloadCompFileList()
 589 |             ElseIf PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionResourcePack Then
 590 |                 '资源包管理自动刷新
 591 |                 If FrmInstanceResourcePack IsNot Nothing Then FrmInstanceResourcePack.ReloadCompFileList()
 592 |             ElseIf PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionShader Then
 593 |                 '光影包管理自动刷新
 594 |                 If FrmInstanceShader IsNot Nothing Then FrmInstanceShader.ReloadCompFileList()
 595 |             ElseIf PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionSchematic Then
 596 |                 '投影原理图管理自动刷新
 597 |                 If FrmInstanceSchematic IsNot Nothing Then FrmInstanceSchematic.ReloadCompFileList()
 598 |             ElseIf PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSelect Then
 599 |                 '实例选择自动刷新
 600 |                 LoaderFolderRun(McInstanceListLoader, PathMcFolder, LoaderFolderRunType.RunOnUpdated, MaxDepth:=1, ExtraPath:="versions\")
 601 |             End If
 602 |         Catch ex As Exception
 603 |             Log(ex, "切回窗口时出错", LogLevel.Feedback)
 604 |         End Try
 605 |     End Sub

 607 |     '文件拖放
 608 |     Private Sub HandleDrag(sender As Object, e As DragEventArgs)
 609 |         Try
 610 |             If e.Handled AndAlso (e.Effects <> DragDropEffects.None) Then Return
 611 |             e.Handled = True
 612 |             '缓存
 613 |             Static PrevData As IDataObject, PrevEffects As DragDropEffects
 614 |             If e.Data Is PrevData Then
 615 |                 e.Effects = PrevEffects
 616 |                 Return
 617 |             End If
 618 |             '确定拖放效果
 619 |             e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None
 620 |             If e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.Text) Then
 621 |                 Dim Str As String = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.Text)
 622 |                 If Str.StartsWithF("authlib-injector:yggdrasil-server:") Then
 623 |                     e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Copy
 624 |                 ElseIf Str.StartsWithF("file:///") Then
 625 |                     e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Copy
 626 |                 End If
 627 |             ElseIf e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop) Then
 628 |                 Dim Files As String() = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop)
 629 |                 If Files IsNot Nothing AndAlso Files.Length > 0 Then
 630 |                     e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Link
 631 |                 End If
 632 |             End If
 633 |             PrevData = e.Data
 634 |             PrevEffects = e.Effects
 635 |             Log("[System] 设置拖放类型：" & GetStringFromEnum(e.Effects))
 636 |         Catch ex As Exception
 637 |             Log(ex, "处理拖放时出错", LogLevel.Feedback)
 638 |         End Try
 639 |     End Sub
 640 |     Private Sub FrmMain_Drop(sender As Object, e As DragEventArgs) Handles Me.Drop
 641 |         Try
 642 |             If e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.Text) Then
 643 |                 '获取文本
 644 |                 Try
 645 |                     Dim Str As String = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.Text)
 646 |                     Log("[System] 接受文本拖拽：" & Str)
 647 |                     If Str.StartsWithF("authlib-injector:yggdrasil-server:") Then
 648 |                         'Authlib 拖拽
 649 |                         e.Handled = True
 650 |                         e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Copy
 651 |                         Dim AuthlibServer As String = Net.WebUtility.UrlDecode(Str.Substring("authlib-injector:yggdrasil-server:".Length))
 652 |                         Log("[System] Authlib 拖拽：" & AuthlibServer)
 653 |                         If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(New ValidateHttp().Validate(AuthlibServer)) Then
 654 |                             Hint($"输入的 Authlib 验证服务器不符合网址格式（{AuthlibServer}）！", HintType.Critical)
 655 |                             Return
 656 |                         End If
 657 |                         If MyMsgBox($"是否要创建新的第三方验证档案？{vbCrLf}验证服务器地址：{AuthlibServer}", "创建新的第三方验证档案", "确定", "取消") = 2 Then Exit Sub
 658 |                         SelectedProfile = Nothing
 659 |                         RunInUi(Sub()
 660 |                                     PageLoginAuth.DraggedAuthServer = AuthlibServer
 661 |                                     FrmLaunchLeft.RefreshPage(True, McLoginType.Auth)
 662 |                                 End Sub)
 663 |                         If PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionSetup Then
 664 |                             '正在服务器选项页，需要刷新设置项显示
 665 |                             FrmInstanceSetup.Reload()
 666 |                         End If
 667 |                     ElseIf Str.StartsWithF("file:///") Then
 668 |                         '文件拖拽（例如从浏览器下载窗口拖入）
 669 |                         Dim FilePath = Net.WebUtility.UrlDecode(Str).Substring("file:///".Length).Replace("/", "\")
 670 |                         e.Handled = True
 671 |                         e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Copy
 672 |                         FileDrag(New List(Of String) From {FilePath})
 673 |                     End If
 674 |                 Catch ex As Exception
 675 |                     Log(ex, "无法接取文本拖拽事件", LogLevel.Developer)
 676 |                     Return
 677 |                 End Try
 678 |             ElseIf e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop) Then
 679 |                 '获取文件并检查
 680 |                 Dim FilePathRaw = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop)
 681 |                 If FilePathRaw Is Nothing Then '#2690
 682 |                     Hint("请将文件解压后再拖入！", HintType.Critical)
 683 |                     Return
 684 |                 End If
 685 |                 e.Handled = True
 686 |                 e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Link
 687 |                 FileDrag(CType(FilePathRaw, IEnumerable(Of String)))
 688 |             End If
 689 |         Catch ex As Exception
 690 |             Log(ex, "接取拖拽事件失败", LogLevel.Feedback)
 691 |         End Try
 692 |     End Sub
 693 |     Private Sub FileDrag(FilePathList As IEnumerable(Of String))
 694 |         RunInNewThread(
 695 |         Sub()
 696 |             Dim FilePath As String = FilePathList.First
 697 |             Log("[System] 接受文件拖拽：" & FilePath & If(FilePathList.Any, $" 等 {FilePathList.Count} 个文件", ""), LogLevel.Developer)
 698 |             '基础检查
 699 |             If Directory.Exists(FilePathList.First) AndAlso Not File.Exists(FilePathList.First) Then
 700 |                 Hint("请拖入一个文件，而非文件夹！", HintType.Critical)
 701 |                 Return
 702 |             ElseIf Not File.Exists(FilePathList.First) Then
 703 |                 Hint("拖入的文件不存在：" & FilePathList.First, HintType.Critical)
 704 |                 Return
 705 |             End If
 706 |             '多文件拖拽
 707 |             If FilePathList.Count > 1 Then
 708 |                 '检查是否为同类型文件
 709 |                 Dim FirstExtension = FilePathList.First.AfterLast(".").ToLower
 710 |                 Dim AllSameType = FilePathList.All(Function(f) f.AfterLast(".").ToLower = FirstExtension)
 711 | 
 712 |                 If AllSameType AndAlso {"jar", "litemod", "disabled", "old", "litematic", "nbt", "schematic", "schem"}.Contains(FirstExtension) Then
 713 |                     '允许同类型的 Mod 文件或投影文件批量拖拽
 714 |                 Else
 715 |                     Hint("一次请只拖入相同类型的文件！", HintType.Critical)
 716 |                     Return
 717 |                 End If
 718 |             End If
 719 |             '主页
 720 |             Dim Extension As String = FilePath.AfterLast(".").ToLower
 721 |             If Extension = "xaml" Then
 722 |                 Log("[System] 文件后缀为 XAML，作为主页加载")
 723 |                 If File.Exists(ExePath & "PCL\Custom.xaml") Then
 724 |                     If MyMsgBox("已存在一个主页文件，是否要将它覆盖？", "覆盖确认", "覆盖", "取消") = 2 Then
 725 |                         Return
 726 |                     End If
 727 |                 End If
 728 |                 CopyFile(FilePath, ExePath & "PCL\Custom.xaml")
 729 |                 RunInUi(
 730 |                 Sub()
 731 |                     Setup.Set("UiCustomType", 1)
 732 |                     FrmLaunchRight.ForceRefresh()
 733 |                     Hint("已加载主页自定义文件！", HintType.Finish)
 734 |                 End Sub)
 735 |                 Return
 736 |             End If
 737 |             '安装 Mod
 738 |             If PageInstanceCompResource.InstallMods(FilePathList) Then Exit Sub
 739 |             '安装投影文件
 740 |             If {"litematic", "nbt", "schematic", "schem"}.Contains(Extension) Then
 741 |                 Log($"[System] 文件为 {Extension} 格式，尝试作为原理图安装")
 742 |                 ' 获取当前文件夹路径（如果在资源管理页面）
 743 |                 Dim targetFolderPath As String = Nothing
 744 |                 If PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionSchematic AndAlso
 745 |                    FrmInstanceSchematic IsNot Nothing AndAlso TypeOf FrmInstanceSchematic Is PageInstanceCompResource Then
 746 |                     targetFolderPath = DirectCast(FrmInstanceSchematic, PageInstanceCompResource).CurrentFolderPath
 747 |                 End If
 748 |                 PageInstanceCompResource.InstallCompFiles(FilePathList, CompType.Schematic, targetFolderPath)
 749 |                 Exit Sub
 750 |             End If
 751 |             '处理资源安装
 752 |             If PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso {"zip"}.Any(Function(i) i = Extension) Then
 753 |                 Select Case PageCurrentSub
 754 |                     Case PageSubType.VersionWorld
 755 |                         Dim DestFolder = PageInstanceLeft.Instance.PathIndie + "saves\" + GetFileNameWithoutExtentionFromPath(FilePath)
 756 |                         If Directory.Exists(DestFolder) Then
 757 |                             Hint("发现同名文件夹，无法粘贴：" + DestFolder, HintType.Critical)
 758 |                             Exit Sub
 759 |                         End If
 760 |                         ExtractFile(FilePath, DestFolder)
 761 |                         Hint($"已导入 {GetFileNameWithoutExtentionFromPath(FilePath)}", HintType.Finish)
 762 |                         If FrmInstanceSaves IsNot Nothing Then RunInUi(Sub() FrmInstanceSaves.Reload())
 763 |                         Exit Sub
 764 |                     Case PageSubType.VersionResourcePack
 765 |                         Dim DestFile = PageInstanceLeft.Instance.PathIndie + "resourcepacks\" + GetFileNameFromPath(FilePath)
 766 |                         If File.Exists(DestFile) Then
 767 |                             Hint("已存在同名文件：" + DestFile, HintType.Critical)
 768 |                             Exit Sub
 769 |                         End If
 770 |                         CopyFile(FilePath, DestFile)
 771 |                         Hint($"已导入 {GetFileNameFromPath(FilePath)}", HintType.Finish)
 772 |                         If FrmInstanceResourcePack IsNot Nothing Then RunInUi(Sub() FrmInstanceResourcePack.ReloadCompFileList())
 773 |                         Exit Sub
 774 |                     Case PageSubType.VersionShader
 775 |                         Dim DestFile = PageInstanceLeft.Instance.PathIndie + "shaderpacks\" + GetFileNameFromPath(FilePath)
 776 |                         If File.Exists(DestFile) Then
 777 |                             Hint("已存在同名文件：" + DestFile, HintType.Critical)
 778 |                             Exit Sub
 779 |                         End If
 780 |                         CopyFile(FilePath, DestFile)
 781 |                         Hint($"已导入 {GetFileNameFromPath(FilePath)}", HintType.Finish)
 782 |                         If FrmInstanceShader IsNot Nothing Then RunInUi(Sub() FrmInstanceShader.ReloadCompFileList())
 783 |                         Exit Sub
 784 |                 End Select
 785 |             End If
 786 |             '处理投影文件
 787 |             If PageCurrent = PageType.InstanceSetup AndAlso {"litematic", "nbt", "schematic", "schem"}.Contains(Extension) AndAlso PageCurrentSub = PageSubType.VersionSchematic Then
 788 |                 Dim DestFile = PageInstanceLeft.Instance.PathIndie + "schematics\" + GetFileNameFromPath(FilePath)
 789 |                 If File.Exists(DestFile) Then
 790 |                     Hint("已存在同名文件：" + DestFile, HintType.Critical)
 791 |                     Exit Sub
 792 |                 End If
 793 |                 Directory.CreateDirectory(PageInstanceLeft.Instance.PathIndie + "schematics\")
 794 |                 CopyFile(FilePath, DestFile)
 795 |                 Hint($"已导入 {GetFileNameFromPath(FilePath)}", HintType.Finish)
 796 |                 If FrmInstanceSchematic IsNot Nothing Then RunInUi(Sub() FrmInstanceSchematic.ReloadCompFileList())
 797 |                 Exit Sub
 798 |             End If
 799 |             '安装整合包
 800 |             If {"zip", "rar", "mrpack"}.Any(Function(t) t = Extension) Then '部分压缩包是 zip 格式但后缀为 rar，总之试一试
 801 |                 Log("[System] 文件为压缩包，尝试作为整合包安装")
 802 |                 Try
 803 |                     ModpackInstall(FilePath)
 804 |                     Return
 805 |                 Catch ex As CancelledException
 806 |                     Return '用户主动取消
 807 |                 Catch ex As Exception
 808 |                     '安装失败，继续往后尝试
 809 |                 End Try
 810 |             End If
 811 |             If {"zip", "rar"}.Any(Function(t) t = Extension) Then
 812 |                 Log("[System] 文件为压缩包，尝试作为存档分析")
 813 |                 Try
 814 |                     ReadWorld(FilePath)
 815 |                     Return
 816 |                 Catch ex As CancelledException
 817 |                     Return '是存档，但是损坏了
 818 |                 Catch ex As Exception
 819 |                     '不是存档（或遇到了其他问题），继续往后尝试
 820 |                 End Try
 821 |             End If
 822 |             '错误报告分析
 823 |             Try
 824 |                 Log("[System] 尝试进行错误报告分析")
 825 |                 Dim Analyzer As New CrashAnalyzer(GetUuid())
 826 |                 Analyzer.Import(FilePath)
 827 |                 If Not Analyzer.Prepare() Then Exit Try
 828 |                 Analyzer.Analyze()
 829 |                 Analyzer.Output(True, New List(Of String))
 830 |                 Return
 831 |             Catch ex As Exception
 832 |                 Log(ex, "自主错误报告分析失败", LogLevel.Feedback)
 833 |             End Try
 834 |             '未知操作
 835 |             Hint("PCL 无法确定应当执行的文件拖拽操作……")
 836 |         End Sub, "文件拖拽")
 837 |     End Sub
 838 | 
 839 |     '接受到 Windows 窗体事件
 840 |     Public IsSystemTimeChanged As Boolean = False
 841 |     Private Function WndProc(hwnd As IntPtr, msg As Integer, wParam As IntPtr, lParam As IntPtr, ByRef handled As Boolean) As IntPtr
 842 |         If msg = 30 Then
 843 |             Dim NowDate = Date.Now
 844 |             If NowDate.Date = ApplicationOpenTime.Date Then
 845 |                 Log("[System] 系统时间微调为：" & NowDate.ToLongDateString & " " & NowDate.ToLongTimeString)
 846 |                 IsSystemTimeChanged = False
 847 |             Else
 848 |                 Log("[System] 系统时间修改为：" & NowDate.ToLongDateString & " " & NowDate.ToLongTimeString)
 849 |                 IsSystemTimeChanged = True
 850 |             End If
 851 |         ElseIf msg = 400 * 16 + 2 Then
 852 |             Log("[System] 收到置顶信息：" & hwnd.ToInt64)
 853 |             If Not IsWindowLoadFinished Then
 854 |                 Log("[System] 窗口尚未加载完成，忽略置顶请求")
 855 |                 Return IntPtr.Zero
 856 |             End If
 857 |             ShowWindowToTop()
 858 |             handled = True
 859 |         ElseIf msg = 26 Then 'WM_SETTINGCHANGE
 860 |             If Marshal.PtrToStringAuto(lParam) = "ImmersiveColorSet" Then
 861 |                 Log($"[System] 系统主题更改，深色模式：{SystemTheme.IsSystemInDarkMode()}")
 862 |                 If Setup.Get("UiDarkMode") = 2 And IsDarkMode <> SystemTheme.IsSystemInDarkMode() Then
 863 |                     IsDarkMode = SystemTheme.IsSystemInDarkMode()
 864 |                     ThemeRefresh()
 865 |                 End If
 866 |             End If
 867 |         End If
 868 | 
 869 |         Return IntPtr.Zero
 870 |     End Function
 871 | 
 872 |     '窗口隐藏与置顶
 873 |     Private _Hidden As Boolean = False
 874 |     Public Property Hidden As Boolean
 875 |         Get
 876 |             Return _Hidden
 877 |         End Get
 878 |         Set(value As Boolean)
 879 |             If _Hidden = value Then Return
 880 |             _Hidden = value
 881 |             If value Then
 882 |                 '隐藏
 883 |                 Left -= 10000
 884 |                 ShowInTaskbar = False
 885 |                 Visibility = Visibility.Hidden
 886 |                 Log("[System] 窗口已隐藏，位置：(" & Left & "," & Top & ")")
 887 |             Else
 888 |                 '取消隐藏
 889 |                 If Left < -2000 Then Left += 10000
 890 |                 ShowWindowToTop()
 891 |             End If
 892 |         End Set
 893 |     End Property
 894 |     '解决龙猫的非通用实现史山
 895 |     Protected Overrides Sub OnActivated(e As EventArgs)
 896 |         MyBase.OnActivated(e)
 897 |         If Hidden Then Hidden = False
 898 |     End Sub
 899 |     ''' <summary>
 900 |     ''' 把当前窗口拖到最前面。
 901 |     ''' </summary>
 902 |     Public Sub ShowWindowToTop()
 903 |         RunInUi(
 904 |         Sub()
 905 |             '这一坨乱七八糟的，别改，改了指不定就炸了，自己电脑还复现不出来
 906 |             Visibility = Visibility.Visible
 907 |             ShowInTaskbar = True
 908 |             WindowState = WindowState.Normal
 909 |             Hidden = False
 910 |             Topmost = True '偶尔 SetForegroundWindow 失效
 911 |             Topmost = False
 912 |             SetForegroundWindow(FrmHandle)
 913 |             Focus()
 914 |             Log($"[System] 窗口已置顶，位置：({Left}, {Top}), {Width} x {Height}")
 915 |         End Sub)
 916 |     End Sub
 917 |     '背景视频循环播放
 918 |     Private Sub VideoEnded(sender As Object, e As RoutedEventArgs)
 919 |         VideoBack.Position = TimeSpan.Zero
 920 |         VideoBack.Play()
 921 |     End Sub
 922 |     '最小化时暂停背景视频
 923 |     Private Sub WindowStateChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles Me.StateChanged
 924 |         Select Case Me.WindowState
 925 |             Case WindowState.Minimized
 926 |                 ModVideoBack.IsMinimized = True
 927 |                 VideoPause()
 928 |             Case WindowState.Normal
 929 |                 ModVideoBack.IsMinimized = False
 930 |                 VideoPlay()
 931 |         End Select
 932 |     End Sub

 936 | #Region "切换页面"
 937 | 
 938 |     '页面种类与属性
 939 |     '注意，这一枚举在 “切换页面” EventType 中调用，应视作公开 API 的一部分
 940 |     ''' <summary>
 941 |     ''' 页面种类。
 942 |     ''' </summary>
 943 |     Public Enum PageType
 944 |         ''' <summary>
 945 |         ''' 启动。
 946 |         ''' </summary>
 947 |         Launch = 0
 948 |         ''' <summary>
 949 |         ''' 下载。
 950 |         ''' </summary>
 951 |         Download = 1
 952 |         ''' <summary>
 953 |         ''' 联机。
 954 |         ''' </summary>
 955 |         Link = 2
 956 |         ''' <summary>
 957 |         ''' 设置。
 958 |         ''' </summary>
 959 |         Setup = 3
 960 |         ''' <summary>
 961 |         ''' 更多。
 962 |         ''' </summary>
 963 |         Other = 4
 964 |         ''' <summary>
 965 |         ''' 实例选择。这是一个副页面。
 966 |         ''' </summary>
 967 |         InstanceSelect = 5
 968 |         ''' <summary>
 969 |         ''' 任务管理。这是一个副页面。
 970 |         ''' </summary>
 971 |         TaskManager = 6

1053 |     ''' <summary>
1054 |     ''' 获取次级页面的名称。若并非次级页面则返回空字符串，故可以以此判断是否为次级页面。
1055 |     ''' </summary>
1056 |     Private Function PageNameGet(Stack As PageStackData) As String
1057 |         Select Case Stack.Page
1058 |             Case PageType.InstanceSelect
1059 |                 Return "实例选择"
1060 |             Case PageType.TaskManager
1061 |                 Return "任务管理"
1062 |             Case PageType.GameLog
1063 |                 Return "实时日志"
1064 |             Case PageType.InstanceSetup
1065 |                 Return "实例设置 - " & If(PageInstanceLeft.Instance Is Nothing, "未知实例", PageInstanceLeft.Instance.Name)
1066 |             Case PageType.CompDetail
1067 |                 Return "资源下载 - " & CType(Stack.Additional(0), CompProject).TranslatedName
1068 |             Case PageType.HelpDetail
1069 |                 Return CType(Stack.Additional(0), HelpEntry).Title
1070 |             Case PageType.SetupJava
1071 |                 Return "Java 管理"
1072 |             Case PageType.VersionSaves
1073 |                 Return $"存档管理 - {GetFolderNameFromPath(Stack.Additional)}"
1074 |             Case PageType.HomePageMarket
1075 |                 Return "主页市场"
1076 |             Case Else
1077 |                 Return ""
1078 |         End Select
1079 |     End Function

1103 |     ''' 当前的子页面。
1104 |     ''' </summary>

1106 |         Get
1107 |             Select Case PageCurrent
1108 |                 Case PageType.Download
1109 |                     If FrmDownloadLeft Is Nothing Then FrmDownloadLeft = New PageDownloadLeft
1110 |                     Return FrmDownloadLeft.PageID
1111 |                 Case PageType.Setup
1112 |                     If FrmSetupLeft Is Nothing Then FrmSetupLeft = New PageSetupLeft
1113 |                     Return FrmSetupLeft.PageID
1114 |                 Case PageType.Other
1115 |                     If FrmOtherLeft Is Nothing Then FrmOtherLeft = New PageOtherLeft
1116 |                     Return FrmOtherLeft.PageID
1117 |                 Case PageType.InstanceSetup
1118 |                     If FrmInstanceLeft Is Nothing Then FrmInstanceLeft = New PageInstanceLeft
1119 |                     Return FrmInstanceLeft.PageID
1120 |                 Case Else

1122 |             End Select
1123 |         End Get
1124 |     End Property
1125 |     ''' <summary>
1126 |     ''' 上层页面的编号堆栈，用于返回。
1127 |     ''' </summary>
1128 |     Public PageStack As New List(Of PageStackData)
1129 |     Public Class PageStackData
1130 | 
1131 |         Public Page As PageType
1132 |         Public Additional As Object
1133 | 
1134 |         Public Overrides Function Equals(other As Object) As Boolean
1135 |             If other Is Nothing Then Return False
1136 |             If TypeOf other Is PageStackData Then
1137 |                 Dim PageOther As PageStackData = other
1138 |                 If Page <> PageOther.Page Then Return False
1139 |                 If Additional Is Nothing Then
1140 |                     Return PageOther.Additional Is Nothing
1141 |                 Else
1142 |                     Return PageOther.Additional IsNot Nothing AndAlso Additional.Equals(PageOther.Additional)
1143 |                 End If
1144 |             ElseIf TypeOf other Is Integer Then
1145 |                 If Page <> other Then Return False
1146 |                 Return Additional Is Nothing
1147 |             Else
1148 |                 Return False
1149 |             End If
1150 |         End Function
1151 |         Public Shared Operator =(left As PageStackData, right As PageStackData) As Boolean
1152 |             Return EqualityComparer(Of PageStackData).Default.Equals(left, right)
1153 |         End Operator
1154 |         Public Shared Operator <>(left As PageStackData, right As PageStackData) As Boolean
1155 |             Return Not left = right
1156 |         End Operator
1157 |         Public Shared Widening Operator CType(Value As PageType) As PageStackData
1158 |             Return New PageStackData With {.Page = Value}
1159 |         End Operator
1160 |         Public Shared Widening Operator CType(Value As PageStackData) As PageType
1161 |             Return Value.Page
1162 |         End Operator
1163 |     End Class
1164 |     Public PageLeft As MyPageLeft, PageRight As MyPageRight
1165 | 
1166 |     '引发实际页面切换的入口
1167 |     Private IsChangingPage As Boolean = False
1168 |     ''' <summary>
1169 |     ''' 切换页面，并引起对应选择 UI 的改变。
1170 |     ''' </summary>
1171 |     Public Sub PageChange(Stack As PageStackData, Optional SubType As PageSubType = PageSubType.Default)
1172 |         If PageNameGet(Stack) = "" Then
1173 |             '切换到主页面
1174 |             PageChangeExit()
1175 |             IsChangingPage = True '防止下面的勾选直接触发了 PageChangeActual
1176 |             CType(PanTitleSelect.Children(Stack), MyRadioButton).SetChecked(True, True, PageNameGet(PageCurrent) = "")
1177 |             IsChangingPage = False
1178 |             Select Case Stack.Page
1179 |                 Case PageType.Download
1180 |                     If FrmDownloadLeft Is Nothing Then FrmDownloadLeft = New PageDownloadLeft
1181 |                     For Each item In FrmDownloadLeft.PanItem.Children
1182 |                         If item.GetType() Is GetType(MyListItem) AndAlso Val(item.tag) = SubType Then
1183 |                             CType(item, MyListItem).SetChecked(True, True, Stack = PageCurrent)
1184 |                             Exit For
1185 |                         End If
1186 |                     Next
1187 |                 Case PageType.Setup
1188 |                     If FrmSetupLeft Is Nothing Then FrmSetupLeft = New PageSetupLeft
1189 |                     CType(FrmSetupLeft.PanItem.Children(SubType), MyListItem).SetChecked(True, True, Stack = PageCurrent)
1190 |                 Case PageType.Other
1191 |                     If FrmOtherLeft Is Nothing Then FrmOtherLeft = New PageOtherLeft
1192 |                     CType(FrmOtherLeft.PanItem.Children(SubType), MyListItem).SetChecked(True, True, Stack = PageCurrent)
1193 |             End Select
1194 |             PageChangeActual(Stack, SubType)
1195 |         Else
1196 |             '切换到次页面
1197 |             Select Case Stack.Page
1198 |                 Case PageType.InstanceSetup
1199 |                     If FrmInstanceLeft Is Nothing Then FrmInstanceLeft = New PageInstanceLeft
1200 |                     For Each item In FrmInstanceLeft.PanItem.Children
1201 |                         If item.GetType() Is GetType(MyListItem) AndAlso Val(item.tag) = SubType Then
1202 |                             CType(item, MyListItem).SetChecked(True, True, Stack = PageCurrent)
1203 |                             Exit For
1204 |                         End If
1205 |                     Next
1206 |                 Case PageType.VersionSaves
1207 |                     If FrmInstanceSavesLeft Is Nothing Then FrmInstanceSavesLeft = New PageInstanceSavesLeft
1208 |                     For Each item In FrmInstanceSavesLeft.PanItem.Children
1209 |                         If item.GetType() Is GetType(MyListItem) AndAlso Val(item.tag) = SubType Then
1210 |                             CType(item, MyListItem).SetChecked(True, True, Stack = PageCurrent)
1211 |                             Exit For
1212 |                         End If
1213 |                     Next
1214 |             End Select
1215 |             PageChangeActual(Stack, SubType)
1216 |         End If
1217 |     End Sub
1218 |     ''' <summary>
1219 |     ''' 通过点击导航栏改变页面。
1220 |     ''' </summary>
1221 |     Private Sub BtnTitleSelect_Click(sender As MyRadioButton, raiseByMouse As Boolean) Handles BtnTitleSelect0.Check, BtnTitleSelect1.Check, BtnTitleSelect2.Check, BtnTitleSelect3.Check, BtnTitleSelect4.Check
1222 |         If IsChangingPage Then Return
1223 |         PageChangeActual(Val(sender.Tag))
1224 |     End Sub
1225 |     ''' <summary>
1226 |     ''' 通过点击返回按钮或手动触发返回来改变页面。
1227 |     ''' </summary>
1228 |     Public Sub PageBack() Handles BtnTitleInner.Click
1229 |         If PageStack.Any() Then
1230 |             PageChangeActual(PageStack(0))
1231 |         Else
1232 |             PageChange(PageType.Launch)
1233 |         End If
1234 |     End Sub
1235 | 
1236 |     '实际处理页面切换
1237 |     ''' <summary>
1238 |     ''' 切换现有页面的实际方法。
1239 |     ''' </summary>
1240 |     Private Sub PageChangeActual(Stack As PageStackData, Optional SubType As PageSubType = -1)
1241 |         If PageCurrent = Stack AndAlso (PageCurrentSub = SubType OrElse SubType = -1) Then Return
1242 |         AniControlEnabled += 1
1243 |         Try
1244 | 
1245 | #Region "子页面处理"
1246 |             Dim PageName As String = PageNameGet(Stack)
1247 |             If PageName = "" Then
1248 |                 '即将切换到一个顶级页面
1249 |                 PageChangeExit()
1250 |             Else
1251 |                 '即将切换到一个子页面
1252 |                 If PageStack.Any Then
1253 |                     '子页面 → 另一个子页面，更新
1254 |                     AniStart({
1255 |                         AaOpacity(LabTitleInner, -LabTitleInner.Opacity, 130),
1256 |                         AaCode(Sub() LabTitleInner.Text = PageName,, True),
1257 |                         AaOpacity(LabTitleInner, 1, 150, 30)
1258 |                     }, "FrmMain Titlebar SubLayer")
1259 |                     If PageStack.Contains(Stack) Then
1260 |                         '返回到更上层的子页面
1261 |                         Do While PageStack.Contains(Stack)
1262 |                             PageStack.RemoveAt(0)
1263 |                         Loop
1264 |                     Else
1265 |                         '进入更深层的子页面
1266 |                         PageStack.Insert(0, PageCurrent)
1267 |                     End If
1268 |                 Else
1269 |                     '主页面 → 子页面，进入
1270 |                     PanTitleInner.Visibility = Visibility.Visible
1271 |                     PanTitleMain.IsHitTestVisible = False
1272 |                     PanTitleInner.IsHitTestVisible = True
1273 |                     PageNameRefresh(Stack)
1274 |                     AniStart({
1275 |                         AaOpacity(PanTitleMain, -PanTitleMain.Opacity, 150),
1276 |                         AaX(PanTitleMain, 12 - PanTitleMain.Margin.Left, 150,, New AniEaseInFluent(AniEasePower.Weak)),
1277 |                         AaOpacity(PanTitleInner, 1 - PanTitleInner.Opacity, 150, 200),
1278 |                         AaX(PanTitleInner, -PanTitleInner.Margin.Left, 350, 200, New AniEaseOutBack),
1279 |                         AaCode(Sub() PanTitleMain.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed,, True)
1280 |                     }, "FrmMain Titlebar FirstLayer")
1281 |                     PageStack.Insert(0, PageCurrent)
1282 |                 End If
1283 |             End If
1284 | #End Region
1285 | 
1286 | #Region "实际更改页面框架 UI"
1287 |             PageLast = PageCurrent
1288 |             PageCurrent = Stack
1289 |             Select Case Stack.Page
1290 |                 Case PageType.Launch '启动
1291 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmLaunchLeft, FrmLaunchRight)
1292 |                 Case PageType.Download '下载
1293 |                     If FrmDownloadLeft Is Nothing Then FrmDownloadLeft = New PageDownloadLeft
1294 |                     'PageGet 方法会在未设置 SubType 时指定默认值，并建立相关页面的实例
1295 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmDownloadLeft, FrmDownloadLeft.PageGet(SubType))
1296 |                 Case PageType.Link '联机
1297 |                     If FrmLinkLeft Is Nothing Then FrmLinkLeft = New PageLinkLeft
1298 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmLinkLeft, FrmLinkLeft.PageGet(SubType))
1299 |                 Case PageType.Setup '设置
1300 |                     If FrmSetupLeft Is Nothing Then FrmSetupLeft = New PageSetupLeft
1301 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmSetupLeft, FrmSetupLeft.PageGet(SubType))
1302 |                 Case PageType.SetupJava 'Java 设置
1303 |                     FrmSetupJava = If(FrmSetupJava, New PageSetupJava)
1304 |                     PageChangeAnim(New MyPageLeft, FrmSetupJava)
1305 |                 Case PageType.Other '更多
1306 |                     If FrmOtherLeft Is Nothing Then FrmOtherLeft = New PageOtherLeft
1307 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmOtherLeft, FrmOtherLeft.PageGet(SubType))
1308 |                 Case PageType.GameLog '实时日志
1309 |                     If FrmLogLeft Is Nothing Then FrmLogLeft = New PageLogLeft
1310 |                     If FrmLogLeft Is Nothing Then FrmLogRight = New PageLogRight
1311 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmLogLeft, FrmLogRight)
1312 |                 Case PageType.InstanceSelect '实例选择
1313 |                     If FrmSelectLeft Is Nothing Then FrmSelectLeft = New PageSelectLeft
1314 |                     If FrmSelectRight Is Nothing Then FrmSelectRight = New PageSelectRight
1315 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmSelectLeft, FrmSelectRight)
1316 |                 Case PageType.TaskManager '任务管理
1317 |                     If FrmSpeedLeft Is Nothing Then FrmSpeedLeft = New PageSpeedLeft
1318 |                     If FrmSpeedRight Is Nothing Then FrmSpeedRight = New PageSpeedRight
1319 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmSpeedLeft, FrmSpeedRight)
1320 |                 Case PageType.InstanceSetup '实例设置
1321 |                     If FrmInstanceLeft Is Nothing Then FrmInstanceLeft = New PageInstanceLeft
1322 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmInstanceLeft, FrmInstanceLeft.PageGet(SubType))
1323 |                 Case PageType.CompDetail 'Mod 信息
1324 |                     If FrmDownloadCompDetail Is Nothing Then FrmDownloadCompDetail = New PageDownloadCompDetail
1325 |                     PageChangeAnim(New MyPageLeft, FrmDownloadCompDetail)
1326 |                 Case PageType.HelpDetail '帮助详情
1327 |                     PageChangeAnim(New MyPageLeft, Stack.Additional(1))
1328 |                 Case PageType.VersionSaves '存档管理
1329 |                     If FrmInstanceSavesLeft Is Nothing Then FrmInstanceSavesLeft = New PageInstanceSavesLeft
1330 |                     PageInstanceSavesLeft.CurrentSave = Stack.Additional
1331 |                     PageChangeAnim(FrmInstanceSavesLeft, FrmInstanceSavesLeft.PageGet(SubType))
1332 |                 Case PageType.HomePageMarket '主页市场
1333 |                     FrmHomepageMarket = If(FrmHomepageMarket, New PageHomePageMarket)
1334 |                     PageChangeAnim(New MyPageLeft, FrmHomepageMarket)
1335 |             End Select
1336 | #End Region
1337 | 
1338 | #Region "设置为最新状态"
1339 |             BtnExtraDownload.ShowRefresh()
1340 |             BtnExtraApril.ShowRefresh()
1341 | #End Region
1342 | 
1343 |             Log("[Control] 切换主要页面：" & GetStringFromEnum(Stack) & ", " & SubType)
1344 |         Catch ex As Exception
1345 |             Log(ex, "切换主要页面失败（ID " & PageCurrent.Page & "）", LogLevel.Feedback)
1346 |         Finally
1347 |             AniControlEnabled -= 1
1348 |         End Try
1349 |     End Sub
1350 |     Private Sub PageChangeAnim(TargetLeft As FrameworkElement, TargetRight As FrameworkElement)
1351 |         AniStop("FrmMain LeftChange")
1352 |         AniStop("PageLeft PageChange") '停止左边栏变更导致的右页面切换动画，防止它与本动画一起触发多次 PageOnEnter
1353 |         AniControlEnabled += 1
1354 |         '清除新页面关联性
1355 |         If Not IsNothing(TargetLeft.Parent) Then TargetLeft.SetValue(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, Nothing)
1356 |         If Not IsNothing(TargetRight) AndAlso Not IsNothing(TargetRight.Parent) Then TargetRight.SetValue(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, Nothing)
1357 |         PageLeft = TargetLeft
1358 |         PageRight = TargetRight
1359 |         '触发页面通用动画
1360 |         CType(PanMainLeft.Child, MyPageLeft).TriggerHideAnimation()
1361 |         CType(PanMainRight.Child, MyPageRight).PageOnExit()
1362 |         AniControlEnabled -= 1
1363 |         '执行动画
1364 |         AniStart({
1365 |             AaCode(
1366 |             Sub()
1367 |                 AniControlEnabled += 1
1368 |                 '把新页面添加进容器
1369 |                 PanMainLeft.Child = PageLeft
1370 |                 PageLeft.Opacity = 0
1371 |                 PanMainLeft.Background = Nothing
1372 |                 AniControlEnabled -= 1
1373 |                 RunInUi(Sub() PanMainLeft_Resize(PanMainLeft.ActualWidth), True)
1374 |             End Sub, 110),
1375 |             AaCode(
1376 |             Sub()
1377 |                 '延迟触发页面通用动画，以使得在 Loaded 事件中加载的控件得以处理
1378 |                 PageLeft.Opacity = 1
1379 |                 PageLeft.TriggerShowAnimation()
1380 |             End Sub, 30, True)
1381 |         }, "FrmMain PageChangeLeft")
1382 |         AniStart({
1383 |             AaCode(
1384 |             Sub()
1385 |                 AniControlEnabled += 1
1386 |                 CType(PanMainRight.Child, MyPageRight).PageOnForceExit()
1387 |                 '把新页面添加进容器
1388 |                 PanMainRight.Child = PageRight
1389 |                 PageRight.Opacity = 0
1390 |                 PanMainRight.Background = Nothing
1391 |                 AniControlEnabled -= 1
1392 |                 RunInUi(Sub() BtnExtraBack.ShowRefresh(), True)
1393 |             End Sub, 110),
1394 |             AaCode(
1395 |             Sub()
1396 |                 '延迟触发页面通用动画，以使得在 Loaded 事件中加载的控件得以处理
1397 |                 PageRight.Opacity = 1
1398 |                 PageRight.PageOnEnter()
1399 |             End Sub, 30, True)
1400 |         }, "FrmMain PageChangeRight")
1401 |     End Sub
1402 |     ''' <summary>
1403 |     ''' 退出子界面。
1404 |     ''' </summary>
1405 |     Private Sub PageChangeExit()
1406 |         If PageStack.Any Then
1407 |             '子页面 → 主页面，退出
1408 |             PanTitleMain.Visibility = Visibility.Visible
1409 |             PanTitleMain.IsHitTestVisible = True
1410 |             PanTitleInner.IsHitTestVisible = False
1411 |             AniStart({
1412 |                 AaOpacity(PanTitleInner, -PanTitleInner.Opacity, 150),
1413 |                 AaX(PanTitleInner, -18 - PanTitleInner.Margin.Left, 150,, New AniEaseInFluent),
1414 |                 AaOpacity(PanTitleMain, 1 - PanTitleMain.Opacity, 150, 200),
1415 |                 AaX(PanTitleMain, -PanTitleMain.Margin.Left, 350, 200, New AniEaseOutBack(AniEasePower.Weak)),
1416 |                 AaCode(Sub() PanTitleInner.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed,, True)
1417 |             }, "FrmMain Titlebar FirstLayer")
1418 |             PageStack.Clear()
1419 |         Else
1420 |             '主页面 → 主页面，无事发生
1421 |         End If
1422 |     End Sub
1423 | 
1424 |     '左边栏改变
1425 |     Private Sub PanMainLeft_SizeChanged(sender As Object, e As SizeChangedEventArgs) Handles PanMainLeft.SizeChanged
1426 |         If Not e.WidthChanged Then Return
1427 |         PanMainLeft_Resize(e.NewSize.Width)
1428 |     End Sub
1429 |     Private Sub PanMainLeft_Resize(NewWidth As Double)
1430 |         Dim Delta As Double = NewWidth - RectLeftBackground.Width
1431 |         If Math.Abs(Delta) > 0.1 AndAlso AniControlEnabled = 0 Then
1432 |             If PanMain.Opacity < 0.1 Then PanMainLeft.IsHitTestVisible = False '避免左边栏指向背景未能完美覆盖左边栏
1433 |             If NewWidth > 0 Then
1434 |                 '宽度足够，显示
1435 |                 AniStart({
1436 |                     AaWidth(RectLeftBackground, NewWidth - RectLeftBackground.Width, 180,, New AniEaseOutFluent(AniEasePower.ExtraStrong)),
1437 |                     AaOpacity(RectLeftShadow, 1 - RectLeftShadow.Opacity, 180),
1438 |                     AaCode(Sub() PanMainLeft.IsHitTestVisible = True, 150)
1439 |                 }, "FrmMain LeftChange", True)
1440 |             Else
1441 |                 '宽度不足，隐藏
1442 |                 AniStart({
1443 |                     AaWidth(RectLeftBackground, -RectLeftBackground.Width, 180,, New AniEaseOutFluent),
1444 |                     AaOpacity(RectLeftShadow, -RectLeftShadow.Opacity, 180),
1445 |                     AaCode(Sub() PanMainLeft.IsHitTestVisible = True, 150)
1446 |                 }, "FrmMain LeftChange", True)
1447 |             End If
1448 |         Else
1449 |             RectLeftBackground.Width = NewWidth
1450 |             PanMainLeft.IsHitTestVisible = True
1451 |             AniStop("FrmMain LeftChange")
1452 |         End If
1453 |     End Sub
1454 | 
1455 | #End Region
1456 | 
1457 | #Region "控件拖动"
1458 | 
1459 |     '在时钟中调用，使得即使鼠标在窗口外松开，也可以释放控件
1460 |     Public Sub DragTick()
1461 |         If DragControl Is Nothing Then Return
1462 |         If Not Mouse.LeftButton = MouseButtonState.Pressed Then
1463 |             DragStop()
1464 |         End If
1465 |     End Sub
1466 |     '在鼠标移动时调用，以改变 Slider 位置
1467 |     Public Sub DragDoing() Handles PanBack.MouseMove
1468 |         If DragControl Is Nothing Then Return
1469 |         If Mouse.LeftButton = MouseButtonState.Pressed Then
1470 |             DragControl.DragDoing()
1471 |         Else
1472 |             DragStop()
1473 |         End If
1474 |     End Sub
1475 |     Public Sub DragStop()
1476 |         '存在其他线程调用的可能性，因此需要确保在 UI 线程运行
1477 |         RunInUi(Sub()
1478 |                     If DragControl Is Nothing Then Return
1479 |                     Dim Control = DragControl
1480 |                     DragControl = Nothing
1481 |                     Control.DragStop() '控件会在该事件中判断 DragControl，所以得放在后面
1482 |                 End Sub)
1483 |     End Sub
1484 | 
1485 | #End Region
1486 |

1488 | 
1489 |     '音乐
1490 |     Private Sub BtnExtraMusic_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles BtnExtraMusic.Click
1491 |         MusicControlPause()
1492 |     End Sub
1493 |     Private Sub BtnExtraMusic_RightClick(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles BtnExtraMusic.RightClick
1494 |         MusicControlNext()
1495 |     End Sub
1496 | 
1497 |     '任务管理
1498 |     Private Sub BtnExtraDownload_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles BtnExtraDownload.Click
1499 |         PageChange(PageType.TaskManager)
1500 |     End Sub
1501 |     Private Function BtnExtraDownload_ShowCheck() As Boolean
1502 |         Return HasDownloadingTask() AndAlso Not PageCurrent = PageType.TaskManager
1503 |     End Function
1504 | 
1505 |     '投降
1506 |     Public Sub AprilGiveup() Handles BtnExtraApril.Click
1507 |         If IsAprilEnabled AndAlso Not IsAprilGiveup Then
1508 |             Hint("=D", HintType.Finish)
1509 |             IsAprilGiveup = True
1510 |             FrmLaunchLeft.AprilScaleTrans.ScaleX = 1
1511 |             FrmLaunchLeft.AprilScaleTrans.ScaleY = 1
1512 |             BtnExtraApril.ShowRefresh()
1513 |         End If
1514 |     End Sub
1515 |     Public Function BtnExtraApril_ShowCheck() As Boolean
1516 |         Return IsAprilEnabled AndAlso Not IsAprilGiveup AndAlso PageCurrent = PageType.Launch
1517 |     End Function
1518 | 
1519 |     '关闭 Minecraft
1520 |     Public Sub BtnExtraShutdown_Click() Handles BtnExtraShutdown.Click
1521 |         Try
1522 |             If McLaunchLoaderReal IsNot Nothing Then McLaunchLoaderReal.Abort()
1523 |             For Each Watcher In McWatcherList
1524 |                 Watcher.Kill()
1525 |             Next
1526 |             Hint("已关闭运行中的 Minecraft！", HintType.Finish)
1527 |         Catch ex As Exception
1528 |             Log(ex, "强制关闭所有 Minecraft 失败", LogLevel.Feedback)
1529 |         End Try
1530 |     End Sub
1531 |     Public Function BtnExtraShutdown_ShowCheck() As Boolean
1532 |         Return HasRunningMinecraft
1533 |     End Function
1534 |

1536 |     Public Sub BtnExtraLog_Click() Handles BtnExtraLog.Click
1537 |         PageChange(PageType.GameLog)
1538 |     End Sub
1539 |     Public Function BtnExtraLog_ShowCheck() As Boolean
1540 |         If FrmLogLeft Is Nothing OrElse FrmLogRight Is Nothing OrElse PageCurrent = PageType.GameLog Then Return False
1541 |         Return FrmLogLeft.ShownLogs.Count > 0
1542 |     End Function
1543 | 
1544 |     ''' <summary>
1545 |     ''' 返回顶部。
1546 |     ''' </summary>
1547 |     Public Sub BackToTop() Handles BtnExtraBack.Click
1548 |         Dim RealScroll As MyScrollViewer = BtnExtraBack_GetRealChild()
1549 |         If RealScroll IsNot Nothing Then
1550 |             RealScroll.PerformVerticalOffsetDelta(-RealScroll.VerticalOffset)
1551 |         Else
1552 |             Log("[UI] 无法返回顶部，未找到合适的 RealScroll", LogLevel.Hint)
1553 |         End If
1554 |     End Sub
1555 |     Private Function BtnExtraBack_ShowCheck() As Boolean
1556 |         Dim RealScroll As MyScrollViewer = BtnExtraBack_GetRealChild()
1557 |         Return RealScroll IsNot Nothing AndAlso RealScroll.Visibility = Visibility.Visible AndAlso RealScroll.VerticalOffset > Height + If(BtnExtraBack.Show, 0, 700)
1558 |     End Function
1559 |     Private Function BtnExtraBack_GetRealChild() As MyScrollViewer
1560 |         If PanMainRight.Child Is Nothing OrElse TypeOf PanMainRight.Child IsNot MyPageRight Then Return Nothing
1561 |         Return CType(PanMainRight.Child, MyPageRight).PanScroll
1562 |     End Function

### 问题分析

这段代码位于 `FormMain_Loaded` 事件处理程序中启动的一个新线程里，具体位置是 [`FormMain.xaml.vb:185-206`](Plain Craft Launcher 2/FormMain.xaml.vb:185-206)。

```vb
'特殊版本提示
#If DEBUG Or DEBUGCI Then
    If Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PCL_DISABLE_DEBUG_HINT") Is Nothing Then
        ' ... (弹窗逻辑)
    End If
#End If
```

它的作用是在 `DEBUG` 或 `DEBUGCI` 编译配置下，弹出一个警告框，提示用户正在使用的是非稳定版本。这个提示可以通过设置一个名为 `PCL_DISABLE_DEBUG_HINT` 的环境变量来禁用。

### 解决方案

要永久移除这个启动提示，最直接的方法就是将整个 `#If DEBUG Or DEBUGCI Then ... #End If` 代码块完全删除或注释掉。

2.  找到第 185 行开始的“特殊版本提示”代码块。
3.  将其完全删除。
